Assigned role: day performs disclosure. Abductive move: I supply the causal arrow that daylight removes a cover from the feminine referent; the packet supplies day/opening/uncovering branches and the later cover root غ ش و. Reading change: the ayah becomes an event of revelation, a functional counterpart to both divine inspiration and public exposure of wrongdoing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B001","root_001559/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1446113b836f876593ab","text":"3. **A controlled opening that exposes the risk of breach.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ن ه ر/B003` is opening or widening until something flows; 91:8 `ف ج ر/B001` is a wide split and outflow, alongside `ف ج ر/B004`, breach of restraint. **Structural cue and role:** the focus window moves from day's opening to a self informed of `فجورها`, linking optical aperture to moral rupture. **Abductive move:** the two opening-images are lexical data; positing controlled disclosure and uncontrolled breach as two outcomes of aperture is inferred. **Reading change:** day becomes a channel of manifestation whose mechanics also make the later danger of boundary-breaking intelligible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000722/B003","root_001132/B001","root_001559/B001","root_001559/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_256f0b222dd3ce0237a4","text":"3. **River/opening branch makes disclosure a channeling of flow.** Lexical evidence: ن ه ر B001 \"river cutting the ground with flowing water\"; ن ه ر B003 \"opening or widening until something flows\"; ف ج ر B001 \"wide splitting and outflow\" in 91:8; س ق ي B003 \"irrigation share and channel\" in 91:13. Structural cue: day/unveiling occurs before fissure/outflow and watering rights. Assigned role: نهار is activated as open channel, making disclosure a path for flow. Abductive move: I supply an ecological analogy between light opening and water channels opening; the packet supplies river/opening, fajr-splitting, and watering-channel branches. Reading change: the ayah is not only visual; it also becomes hydraulic, opening channels through which life or breach will later run.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000256/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6a6b7e103462ce3c0606","text":"2. **Perceptual polishing before inner discrimination.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ج ل و/B002` is polishing a blade or clearing sight; 91:7 `ن ف س/B013` is inner mind and discernment, followed in 91:8 by the paired contents given to it. **Structural cue and role:** outward clearing precedes the shaping and informing of an inward perceiver, so polishing serves as a material model for legibility. **Abductive move:** the packet gives outer disclosure followed by inner discrimination; the arrow from clarified perception to readable moral distinction is analogical and inferred. **Reading change:** `جلاها` acquires the force of sharpening the capacity to see, not only increasing illumination.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001559/B001","root_001559/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_85eda86ecb7d8b42b679","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:3:1\",\"root_001559\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gündüzün açılması, ışığın yanı sıra akışa yol veren bir kanal gibi çalışır; bu özellik ilerideki taşma ve su payı düzenini önceden biçimlendirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001088/B001","root_001559/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_95e7829076925f9e15bb","text":"1. **Day as an active disclosure operation.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ن ه ر/B002` is day opening with light and fixed `ج ل و/B001` is uncovering after concealment; 91:4 answers with `غ ش و/B001`, a covering veil. **Structural cue and role:** the matched constructions `إذا جلاها` and `إذا يغشاها` reverse one another around the same suffix, assigning day the role of disclosure rather than background time. **Abductive move:** the grammatical reversal is explicit; treating the two clauses as opposed operations on one visible field is inferred. **Reading change:** day does not merely coincide with visibility; it actively makes the referent manifest.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_99154fb629f132fedb28","text":"91:13's سُقياها and 91:14's بذنبهم made the river/opening branch of ن ه ر more active than a simple day/night contrast. Structural cue: repeated `ها` links the cosmic object across sun, moon, day, night, sky, earth, soul. Assigned role: the bridal-display variant makes manifestation relational and guarded, not just optical. Abductive move: I infer a transferred social scene: something is being presented for recognition; the packet supplies the repeated feminine pronoun and the display branch.
